Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any complexity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 337. The projective resolution has length at most 135 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_337(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 337. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^135(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
